## [1.76.0] - 2026-10-15 — pre-merge-completeness-gate: pre_merge 前置 checkpoint 完整性门 (`10CG/Aria#199`; MINOR: 新脚本 + 新输入面 + 新 stdout 契约, 行为变更见迁移十三条)

### Added — audit-engine `completeness_gate.py` (pre_merge 专属前置完整性门)

- 新脚本 `skills/audit-engine/scripts/completeness_gate.py` (stdlib-only): 在调用任何审计 Agent 之前, 对本次 PR 的作用域逐 (checkpoint, change_id) 做三态判定 (present / missing / not_applicable), 全部非 missing 才放行 (exit 0); 一对 missing 即整体 fail (exit 1)。
- 五个输入参数: `--repo-path` (锚点仓根) / `--diff-repo-path` (diff 仓根; 同仓形态显式传与 `--repo-path` 相同的值) / `--base` (diff 仓主干远程跟踪 ref, 无缺省) / `--anchor-base` (锚点仓主干远程跟踪 ref, 缺省 = `--base`) / `--change-id` (显式作用域, 可重复; 与 `--no-spec` 互斥)。
- 16 键 stdout JSON 契约 (含 `elapsed_ms` 墙钟毫秒, `int`); 报告归属按文件名以 `-<change_id>` 结尾的末段界定, 他 change 的报告与子目录内的报告不计入; `[OK]` / `[INFO]` / ERROR 三种人读行。

### Changed — 完整性检查 Step 3-5 改为按 change 匹配与三态

- `audit-engine` 的完整性检查 (execution-modes.md 与 SKILL.md 的 Step 3-5) 从「glob 文件名 + 目录里有报告即过」改为「经 completeness_gate.py 按 (checkpoint, change_id) 三态判定」; Phase A-only (diff 全落 `openspec/changes/<change>/` 与 `.aria/audit-reports/` 下) 的 post_implementation 判 not_applicable 并以 `[INFO]` surface。
- `phase-c-integrator/SKILL.md` 步骤 5 接线: 门 pass 而精确 off 的跳过分支保留; 同文件另两处同形位置与 `audit-engine/SKILL.md` 的 pre_merge 启用判读一并改为按 C.2 pre_hook 步骤 3 的优先级链 (config 显式值 > `adaptive_rules` 推导值)。

### 迁移 (行为变更十三条 — 全部仅影响 `audit.enabled=true ∧ checkpoints.pre_merge != off` 的采用方; 本仓不在其中)

1. 曾经的假绿 (他 change 的报告、子目录内报告被误计入) 会变成 `missing` 阻断 —— 这是修复本身。
2. `allow_incomplete_checkpoints` 的豁免面收窄: 仍逐对评估三态并全部留痕, 只把 missing / scope_unresolved / spec_level_undetermined / no_spec_unverifiable 降为 `bypassed` (exit 0) 并短路终止求值; `no_spec_contradicted` / `change_id_unanchored` 不被本键豁免。
3. 空纳入集分格处置: 官方 config-example 场景 A (manual + 仅 pre_merge 显式开) 与 legacy `agent_team_audit_points: ["pre_merge"]` 仍 pass, 仅新增一行 `[INFO] 无前置 checkpoint 纳入校验` 留痕 (零行为变化); adaptive 档 Level 1 change 落同判的格 D (`[INFO] 文案不同`); `audit.enabled != true` 或 `pre_merge == off` 时脚本被直调, 从静默 pass 变为 exit 2 (`audit_not_enabled` / `pre_merge_not_enabled` —— 生产路径有调用方守卫, 不可达)。
4. `allow_dangling_change_ids` 继承到 S1 锚点校验 (新增): 开该键的迁移期采用方, 锚点缺失只记 `[WARN]` 并按零报告继续评估, 不 exit 2。
5. 坏 config JSON: `config_unreadable` exit 2 (fail-closed), 刻意不同于 config-loader 散文的「警告 + 返回默认值」—— 否则损坏的 config 会静默落成空纳入集。
6. adaptive 档 Level 解析失败 (proposal 无可解析 Level 行): `spec_level_undetermined` exit 2; 逃生口 = `allow_incomplete_checkpoints` (降 bypassed) 或 `allow_dangling_change_ids` (降 `[WARN]` 并跳过 adaptive 推导)。
7. `adaptive_rules` 推导的 checkpoint 首次真的进入校验面 (改前散文按字面键逐 key): 场景 B/C 采用方会新看到 `missing` —— 这是修复本身 (该跑没跑原查不出); 生产路径可达性由调用方步骤 3 同步改读优先级链保证。
8. `audit.mode` 取值不在四值枚举内: 从静默 off (空纳入集盖章 pass) 变为 exit 2 (fail-closed)。
9. 门首次引入 git 依赖: `--base` / `--anchor-base` 解析失败或 merge-base 失败一律 `git_failed` exit 2 —— 浅克隆 / 无远程跟踪 ref / 容器内无 git 的环境从「静默通过」变硬阻。
10. 跨仓 + Level 1 (`--no-spec`) 且锚点面零 diff: `no_spec_unverifiable` —— 可由 `allow_incomplete_checkpoints` 豁免降 `bypassed` (exit 0); `allow_dangling_change_ids` 不覆盖。
11. adaptive 档新纳入 checkpoint 的报告在产出侧调用链上不产出 ⇒ 这批采用方会拿到新的假红: ERROR 模板的 Fix 清单已补可执行条 (在 `audit.checkpoints` 里**显式写**该 checkpoint); 产出侧四个调用点的接线改造另有跟踪 issue (号在 Phase D 开出后记周期 handoff)。
12. audit-engine 入口第二道启用守卫改读优先级链 + adaptive 档取上界: 场景 B/C 采用方的 pre_merge 从「门从不跑」变成「会跑」。
13. `post_brainstorm` 不再作为前置依赖 (checkpoint 排除五项之一, 与 mid_post_spec 等同列)。

Rule #6: 两个套件照跑 (`ab-suite/audit-engine.json` delta.pass_rate +0.27, 定向 eval 3 without 臂 1/5 < with 臂 5/5; `phase-c-integrator` 套件逐 eval 零回归), 结果 `aria-plugin-benchmarks/ab-results/2026-10-15-pre-merge-completeness-gate-rule6/` (10CG/Aria 主仓)。

## [1.75.0] - 2026-10-15 — secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths: 检测网补洞 + 旁路收口 (`10CG/aria-plugin#154` / `10CG/aria-plugin#203` / `10CG/Aria#221`; MINOR: 新检测能力 + 新输入面 + 新拦截族, 向下兼容)

### Security — PostToolUse secret-scan (检测 + 告警, 不改写输出)

**背景**: 2026-08-20 事故的 JSON `token` 键与 2026-09-26 事故的 `JWT_SECRET = <值>` (等号两侧带空格) 都从全部既有模式底下穿过; 更深的前置缺口是提取链只认 `output` / `content` / `stdout`, 而 Claude Code 2.1.285 的 Read 结果是 `{type:"text", file:{content,…}}` —— **生产中 Read 从未被扫描** (旧 Read 用例用合成形状, 所以一直绿)。

**修复**:
- 提取链补 `.tool_response.file.content` (Read) 与 `.error` (失败调用); NUL 分隔的内容 (`/proc/<pid>/environ`、`env -0`) 另加第二个视图 (含 NUL 的输入在两个视图上各扫一遍, 同一凭据的计数可能多一份, 只会多不会少)。
- `hooks/hooks.json` 为 secret-scan 增一条 `PostToolUseFailure` 注册 (matcher、命令、超时同其 `PostToolUse` 条目; 其余注册零改动): 失败的工具调用 (Bash 非零退出等) 不触发 `PostToolUse`, 它们的错误文本此前整体失明。输出里的 `hookEventName` 取输入的 `hook_event_name`, 因为 harness 按实际触发的事件校验它。
- 6 个通用键形 tag: `json-credential-field` / `json-env-secret-key` / `kv-secret-assign` (INI / env / YAML 赋值, 含等号两侧空格、引号、`export`) / `auth-header-token` / `cf-access-client-secret` / `cli-secret-flag`。值先过分类器: placeholder 白名单 (FAKE / PLACEHOLDER / NOT-REAL / `[REDACTED…]` 整值、`<…>`、`${…}`、`{{…}}`、掩码、以 `…` 结尾的截断值)、熵下限 (小写 / 大写 / 数字至少两类)、路径形、点分标识符链 (如 `process.env.X`)。
- 告警文案 (`additionalContext`) 多一段事实陈述: 命中的 tag 与来源工具 (`tags: …; source: …`); 不含 `tool_input` 的任何文本。
- 日志 `secret-scan.log` 每行多一个 `fp=` 字段: 每个计入的 span 一个指纹 (值 ≥16 字符取 sha256 前 8 位, 更短只记长度; 至多 10 个), 不含值。
- 两个测试套件把 `HOME` 指向自建临时目录 (不再往真实 `~/.claude/logs` 追加夹具事件), 既有夹具改为运行时拼装。

### Security — PreToolUse secret-guard (按命令文本拦截)

- **`app.ini`**: Forgejo / Gitea 服务端配置 (`JWT_SECRET` / `SECRET_KEY` / `INTERNAL_TOKEN` 与数据库口令都在其中) 进 Bash 面 (29 个打印型读取器 + `python3 -c` / `node -e`) 与 Read/Edit 面, tight credit。
- **进程表**: 带完整命令行列的 `ps` (`ps aux` / `ps -ef` / `ps -eo pid,args` 等)、`pgrep -a`、`pstree -a`、`top -c`、`docker top`、`docker ps --no-trunc`、`/proc/<pid>/cmdline|environ` (pid 位置放宽到 `self` / `$$` / glob / 变量)、11 个远程与外壳包裹下的 `env` / `printenv` 转储。
- **项目扩展入口 `.aria/secret-guard.paths`** (新输入): 一行一个字面子串, 大小写不敏感, **只增不减** (内建先判; 复用内建的同一判定函数, 不另写状态机); 限额 32 KiB / 200 条 / 每条命令前 64 处出现; 失效即**整体忽略且静默**; 保护边界与「条目选最短特征子串」的建议见 `README.md` / `README.zh.md` 与 standards `secret-hygiene.md` §5.6。
- **变量间接 (`partial` 修复)**: 字面赋值 (`f=…; cat "$f"`、`for f in …`、单层链式) 在内建判定全部放行之后再判一遍替换后的副本; 数组、`read <<<`、引号夹在路径中间、`cd` 后相对名、glob、`set --`、`$(printf …)`、跨两次工具调用的变量不覆盖, 由 secret-scan 在输出侧兜底。
- **两处误拦修复 (新放行)**: `python3 -c` / `node -e` / `lua -e` 里 `os.environ` / `os.environb` 的**单键读取** (`.get(` / `[ ]`, 与基线放行的 `os.getenv(` 同一信息量); jq 的 `keys_unsorted` 与 `map_values(length)` (锚定写法)。整表导出与遍历、`.env` 家族经解释器读取**仍拦**。
- **拒绝文案零改动** (BLOCKED 文本归一化后与基线逐字节一致): 「凭据不要放命令行参数」的建议落 standards `secret-hygiene.md` §3.8, 不进共用文案。已知不一致: 共用文案的 `Acceptable filters` 对 tight 族无效 (已记入 SOT §2.5 注与 hook 头注释)。

### 行为变更双向申报

- **新拦截**: `app.ini` 族 (含 `app.ini.bak` 一类备份与模板文件名); 项目扩展条目; 变量间接展开后命中的读取; 进程表族 —— 含以往放行的日常检查习惯 `ps aux | grep …` (及 `ps -aux`)、取 PID 的 `ps aux | grep X | awk '{print $2}'` (替代: `pgrep -f …`)、`ps axo pid,comm`、`ps aux | grep -c X`; 包裹下带变量名的 `printenv <NAME>` (以往放行的容器内单变量核对由放变拦, 改走 `# guard:ack`)。文本提及的误拦 (含 `ssh` / `watch` 这类包裹词再出现进程表形态的提交信息、`echo`) 见 SOT §2.5。**新拦截在 Layer 2 (aria-runner 容器, 无人值守) 里同样生效**: BLOCKED 文案里的 `# guard:ack: <理由>` 在那里由执行方 AI 自己写、没有人复议, 本次不改这一既有机制。
- **新告警**: Read 结果首次被扫描 (读含逼真示例值的文件会告警); 失败调用的错误文本 (`PostToolUseFailure`) 首次被扫描 (该事件上 `additionalContext` 的端到端送达待 post-ship 验证); NUL 分隔内容首次可被检出; 6 个新 tag (含已知误报类, 如文档里以 ASCII `...` 截短的示例值)。
- **新放行**: `os.environ` 单键读取; jq `keys_unsorted` / `map_values(length)`。
- **新静默**: 既有 `json-secret-field` 上的整值占位 / 掩码 / wrapper 占位 / 以 `…` 结尾的截断值; 哨兵回灌造成的重复计数。
- **日志**: `fp=` 字段 (仓内无读取方)。
- **性能 (CPU)**: Read 面每次多一次扫描 (整进程 CPU 时间约 0.104 s → 约 0.299 s; 60 KB 无命中文本约 0.345 s); Bash 面无扩展文件时约 +24% CPU、Read 面约 +7%; 含 `$VAR` 引用的命令因变量间接的第二遍约为基线的 1.45–2.5 倍 (内建先判, 已被拦下的命令不付第二遍); 200 条扩展条目约 +30 ms / 次调用; 命令里出现列入的路径时多付一遍内建判定 (约 2 倍, 与出现点数无关); 长单行 L3 输入的匹配与取值对长度线性。以上为 Spec 原型量级; TASK-010 实测 (268da8f→目标, 交错 CPU min/5): f 1.55–1.72× · g 1.04–1.13× · fh 1.99× · i 1.15–1.23× · i3 固有 1.14–1.74× / 300KB 不带引号 kv 3.04–3.41×; W14 全部 20 个有界档 PASS。

### Documentation

- standards `conventions/secret-hygiene.md` 1.1.2 → 1.2.0 (additive): §2.2 `app.ini` 行 / §2.5 进程表行与 tight 族注 / 新增 §3.8「长时进程的凭据传递」(命令行参数不得含凭据, 与短命令以 60 秒分界; §3.1–§3.7 与 §4 的短命令示例逐字节不变) / §5.1 勘正 (secret-guard 是 Bash + Read/Edit blocker) / §5.2 追加 `updatedToolOutput` 事实 / 新增 §5.6 项目扩展入口; 三处 secret-guard 自测计数与一处 secret-scan 计数同步。
- `README.md` / `README.zh.md` 的 Hooks 用法小节: `.aria/secret-guard.paths` 用法 (字面子串、最短特征子串、保护边界)、`PostToolUseFailure`、`updatedToolOutput` 事实各一句。
- 两个 hook 的头注释勘正 (只改注释, 去掉整行注释后与改前逐字节相同): 去掉陈旧的 `~100` / `49` / `~15` 数量字面与不存在的 argon2 声明; 改写与 `updatedToolOutput` 实测矛盾的「无字段可替换」一句; 补 Read 的 `file.content` 形状与失败事件的 `error` 契约; 记本 change-id。`VERSION` 里 v1.24.0 条目的「output REDACT」改为 detect + warn。

### Tests

- `secret-guard.test.sh` 599 (593 无 zsh) → **822 (816 无 zsh)**, 含 census `family_count` 61 → 64 与新族探针、未绑定变量注入自测、测试内 SC-8 五档延迟闸 (阈值与口径不改); `risky_patterns` 静态行数 149 (预算 ≤ 150)。`secret-scan.test.sh` 49 → **129**, 头注释 `Coverage: K cases` 与一条读取它的自检断言 (K 与实跑总数一致)。standards `secret-hygiene.md` 的计数与二者同步。
- 验收探针 `baseline_probe.py` (WPA_BASH32 + WPA_REQUIRE_ALL=1) 目标态全部 `yes`, PROBE_EXIT=0 target shape holds (baseline-failing 226 · doc-sync 26 · reverse-guard 82 · allow-guard 70 · known-limit 40 · zero-regression 9; 30a = 另跑的 git-history 腿); SC-30 真 checkout guard 816/816 · scan 129/129; W14 时档 20/20 有界档 PASS。

### Notes

- **未做 (范围外)**: L3 升级为「检测 + 脱敏」(`updatedToolOutput`, 产品级, 端到端未验证); Grep 工具读敏感路径 (两层 matcher 都不含 Grep, 结构上无法在本次修); 凭据轮换 (决策单另行处理); WP-B (`10CG/Aria#223` + `10CG/aria-plugin#207`)。
- **覆盖关系**: `10CG/aria-plugin#154` 全覆盖; `10CG/aria-plugin#203` 部分覆盖 (服务端配置 + 扩展入口; 变量间接为 `partial` 修复, 输出侧由 secret-scan 兜底); `10CG/Aria#221` 部分覆盖 (进程表列举与两处误拦已做; 「拒绝文案加一句」改落 SOT)。
- **post-ship 复验** (发版后, owner 更新插件缓存并重启会话后执行): 惰性诱饵验证 Read 提取、进程表与 `app.ini` 拦截、NUL 前提、失败事件送达。

rule6_note: hook bash 代码 + 测试 + 头注释 / 计数 / SOT 文档与 README, 零 SKILL.md、零 `description`、零 `references/` 变更 → 不属 Skill 变更 (`decision_table_row: n/a`, `description_changed: no`), 验证口径为 substitute: 各 SC 的 baseline-failing 行 (基线实跑全红) + 两个套件回归 + 对 canonical hook 的直调探针 + 发版后经 harness hook 链的 post-ship 复验。唯一改动 AI 可见文本的 hunk 是 secret-scan 告警 `additionalContext` 里插入的「tags + source」事实陈述 (判据表第 1 行, 描述性: 不含指示行为的措辞, 不含 `tool_input` 文本; 可证伪锚点 = 插入段之外的全串与基线逐字节相等); L1 的 BLOCKED 文案零改动。该分类为 AI 依 `skill-benchmark-exemption.md` 自行判定, 已列入 Spec「待 owner 复议」。

## [1.74.1] - 2026-09-30 — issue_scan 翻页取全并显式标注截断 (`10CG/aria-plugin#182`; PATCH: 缺陷修复, 新增字段均为 additive)

### Fixed
- **state-scanner `collectors/issue_scan.py` — open issue 超过 `limit` 的仓被静默截断**: 每个仓只发一次 `limit=20` 的请求, 并把 `len(items)` 当 `open_count`; Forgejo 按新→老返回, 丢掉的恒是最老的单。实测 scanner 报 48, 同一时刻 API 分页全量 130 (`10CG/Aria` 20/46, `10CG/aria-plugin` 20/76), 被藏的含泄露类 issue。现在 Forgejo 翻页直到服务端返回**空页**; GitHub 一次 `gh issue list --limit 1001` (上限 1000 + 1 行探针)。
- **旧缓存不再被当完整数据服务**: `issue_status.schema_version` 1.1 → 1.2, reader 只接受 1.2 —— 1.0 / 1.1 缓存至多 `limit` 条且无法判断是否被截断, 升级后一次性重新拉取 (否则 15 分钟 TTL 内继续服务截断结果)。

### Added
- `issue_status.truncated` (顶层) 与 `issue_status.repos[*].truncated` / `truncated_reason` (**恒存在**, 含失败条目): 列举是否提前结束。`truncated_reason` 封闭集: `max_items` (触及单 repo 1000 条安全上限, 按整页取整) / `pagination_stalled` (服务端整页返回已见过的条目, 即无视 `page`)。触顶时 `open_count` 只是下界。
- 测试: `tests/test_issue_scan_pagination.py` (新建, 23 条; 未改生产代码时 22 红 1 绿) + `tests/_helpers.py` 共享谓词 `past_last_issues_page` (三个只 mock 首页的测试替身共用)。

### Changed
- **`limit` 由事实上的总量上限改为每页条数** (Forgejo 夹到 1..50; GitHub 不使用该值)。既有的 `limit: 20` 配置继续有效, 结果从「最新 20 条」变为「全部」。
- 首页请求 URL 与修复前逐字节相同 (无 `page=`), 第 2 页起追加 `&page=N`; 终点判据是**空页**而非「短页」(服务端页大小可被夹小, 短页不等于最后一页); 重叠页按 issue 号去重。
- 首页之后某一页失败 → 整个 repo 记 `fetch_error`, 不返回残缺列表 (与修复前单次请求失败的粒度一致)。
- **触碰的文档面**: `references/state-snapshot-schema.md` (issue_status 字段 + 「Pagination and truncation」小节) / `references/issue-scanning.md` (v1.2 小节 / 缓存兼容表 / `limit` 配置行 / prose 手工路径只取首页的说明) / `config-loader/SKILL.md` (`limit` 语义两行 YAML 注释)。
- **已知边界**:
  1. 触顶上限按整页取整 (`1000 // 页大小`), 页大小不能整除 1000 时实际保留略少于 1000 条; 标注本身仍准确 (探针页保证)。
  2. GitHub 大仓 (数百条 issue) 的 `gh` 单次调用受 `api_timeout_seconds` 约束, 可能由「静默截断」变为 `timeout` (显式失败)。
  3. 手工 prose 路径 (`mechanical_mode=false`, 计划移除) 仍只取首页, 不写 `truncated`; 以 `scan.py` 为准。
  4. 没有加 `10CG/aria-plugin#182` 建议的 `total_available` (默认取全后恒等于 `open_count`, 冗余) 与「取样规则可配置」(已无 cap, 无对象)。

### Notes
- **同形缺陷未在本版处理**: `issue-triage` 的 `collectors/_inflight.py` (开放 PR 列表) 与 `collectors/_issue.py` (评论列表) 同为单次 `limit=50` 请求, 超过 50 条会静默丢数据 —— 跟踪见 `10CG/aria-plugin#208`。
- **Rule #6**: 判据表第 1 行 (描述性 + 纯 collector 代码, 先例 v1.60.0) ⇒ substitute: 结构化 baseline-failing 测试 (新增 23 条, 基线 22 红) + 10 种坏实现变异全被抓 + 真实 Forgejo 两态负控 (完整态 130 = API 全量; 触顶态标注出现 / 消失), **未跑 AB** (`description_changed: no`; 套件对 collector 输出零覆盖, 见 `10CG/aria-plugin#205`)。唯一碰到 SKILL.md 的一处是 `config-loader/SKILL.md` 两行注释, 按「事实性同步」归类。该归类为 AI 依 `skill-benchmark-exemption.md` §2 自行判定, 已交接请 owner 复议。
- 全插件 `skills/run_all_tests.sh`: 11 OK / 0 FAIL / 0 SKIP, 共 2205 个测试。

++ b/hooks/secret-guard.sh
#   by Claude (the LLM) in response to operator prompts. It covers the
#     they flow to LLM context unguarded. The companion PostToolUse hook
#     secret-scan.sh scans the output after the fact (detect + warn only, it
#     cannot undo the leak) — see its header and secret-hygiene.md §5.
#   standards/conventions/secret-hygiene.md §1 principles) it's a
# === Project extension + residual gaps (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths) ===
#
# Extension: <project root>/.aria/secret-guard.paths adds blocks without touching
#   this file. One LITERAL substring per line (case-insensitive, `#` comments, 4-200
#   bytes per entry, at most 200 entries, file at most 32 KiB); the project root is
#   CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, else the stdin cwd. It can only ADD: every built-in verdict
#   comes first, and the first 64 occurrences of an entry in one command are checked.
#   A missing or invalid file turns the extension off SILENTLY (the built-in list is
#   unaffected). Coverage = the readers of the built-in app.ini row + Read/Edit; the
#   Grep tool, redirected reads, `source`, `dd`, `xargs cat`, `find -exec` and the
#   65th occurrence onwards are not covered. List the SHORTEST distinctive substring
#   (a file name), not a full path. Details: secret-hygiene.md §5.6.
# Residual gaps, all unfixed on purpose:
#   - `systemctl status` and `docker inspect <container>` (full JSON with Config.Env)
#     are allowed: common health checks, the false-positive cost is too high.
#     Credential-shaped lines in their output are alerted after the fact by
#     secret-scan.sh (failed calls too).
#   - The shared BLOCKED text lists `Acceptable filters` such as `| grep '^SAFE_PREFIX='`.
#     The tight families (claude-config, app.ini, the ps / pgrep -a / top -c /
#     docker top rows, extension entries) give no credit to line filters, so a
#     retry that follows the text is blocked again; the text is unchanged on
#     purpose (secret-hygiene.md §2.5, note).
#   - Shell variable indirection (`f=...; cat "$f"`) is covered only in part: literal
#     assignments are expanded in a second pass (at most 16 assignments and 8 replacements
#     per variable; beyond those COUNTS the variable passes). The SIZE of the expansion is
#     bounded as well -- a value up to 4096 characters, a substituted segment up to 8192
#     characters and 65536 characters of substituted text per command -- and beyond a SIZE
#     limit the whole command is BLOCKED as too large to scan ("split it", fail-closed,
#     owner ruling 2026-10-14), never passed. Arrays, `read <<<`, quote-spliced paths, `cd`
#     + a relative name, globs, `set --`, `$(printf ...)` and variables across two tool
#     calls pass; the output side is left to secret-scan.sh.
#
#   secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths (10CG/aria-plugin#154, #203; 10CG/Aria#221):
#         (1) Forgejo / Gitea app.ini joins the manifest on BOTH planes (Bash
#             readers, python3 -c / node -e, Read/Edit path regex) with tight credit.
#         (2) Process-table enumeration rows: ps with full command lines, pgrep -a,
#             top -c, docker top, wrapped env dumps, /proc/<pid>/environ|cmdline.
#         (3) Project extension .aria/secret-guard.paths (above): literal substrings,
#             judged by the built-in app.ini judgement, can only add blocks.
#         (4) Shell-variable indirection, a second pass after every built-in verdict
#             allowed (partial coverage, see the residual gaps above).
#         (5) Two false positives removed: os.environ single-key reads and the
#             metadata-only jq forms keys_unsorted / map_values(length).
#         The BLOCKED text is unchanged (the advice on credentials in argv lives in
#         secret-hygiene.md §3.8, not in this text).
# ── secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths (W8 / W11): shared names, single definition, several consumers ──
# W8: server-side config files of Forgejo / Gitea (JWT_SECRET, SECRET_KEY, INTERNAL_TOKEN live in app.ini; 10CG/aria-plugin#203). A path component holding
# `forgejo` or `gitea` (a word-internal prefix is fine: `git-forgejo`), then any non-blank non-quote characters of the same path, then `app.ini`; or
# `custom/conf/app.ini`. The right boundary (no letter / digit / `_` right after `app.ini`: the backup `app.ini.bak` is covered, `app.inix` is not) is added by
# each consumer. Consumers: the Bash-face reader row, the python3 -c / node -e source groups, the credit tightening below; the Read/Edit-face regex spells the
# same group in lower case. No \b anywhere in the new text (BSD regex does not support it, same family as 10CG/aria-plugin#144).
_SG_SRVCFG='[A-Za-z0-9._-]*(forgejo|gitea)[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"']*app\.ini|custom/conf/app\.ini'
# The printing readers of the W8 row: the 13 of the claude-config row plus nl tac rev sort uniq cut paste diff cmp comm xxd od hexdump base64 column fold (29).
_SG_READERS_PRINT='cat|grep|egrep|fgrep|rg|head|tail|less|more|strings|awk|sed|jq|nl|tac|rev|sort|uniq|cut|paste|diff|cmp|comm|xxd|od|hexdump|base64|column|fold'
# W11: process-table enumeration that prints argv / environ -- ps (procps semantics) in its argv-exposing spellings, pgrep / pstree `-a`, top `-c`, docker top,
# docker ps --no-trunc -- at a command position behind an optional launcher / absolute directory prefix, and behind remote / shell wrappers. The three rule rows in
# risky_patterns spell the same text as literals (a rule row that is only a variable is an empty string for the census, SC-31 31c); the copies below are what the
# credit tightening matches. _SG_PROCTAB_ALL is the tight copy: the ps family ONLY -- the env / printenv alternatives of the wrapper row exist in the rule row
# alone, they keep the baseline credit (a dump followed by `| cut -d= -f1` or `| wc -l` stays allowed). The two copies must not drift (SC-22 22bm / 22bn).
_SG_PROCTAB='(^|[;&|(){]|`|[[:cntrl:]])[[:blank:]]*((then|do|else|elif)[[:space:]]+)?((/[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"'`$()<>]*/)?(sudo|doas|nice|timeout|xargs|nohup|stdbuf|env|time|eval|setsid|ionice|unbuffer|command|exec)([[:space:]]+(-[^[:space:]]+([[:space:]]+[^-[:space:]|;&()<>`"'"'"'][^[:space:]|;&()<>`"'"'"']*)?|[0-9][0-9.]*[smhd]?|[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*=[^[:space:]]*))*[[:space:]]+)?((/[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"'`$()<>]*/)?(ps[[:blank:]]+([a-zA-Z]+([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|[0-9]+([^[:alnum:]_.-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*[fF]|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*[oO]|--format)[[:blank:]=]*[^[:blank:]|;&]*(args|cmd|command)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*O|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*x[a-zA-Z]*([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*u[[:blank:]]*($|[|;&)<>]))|pgrep[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--list-full)|pstree[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--arguments)|top[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*c))'
_SG_PS_REMOTE_T='(ssh|docker[[:space:]]+(compose[[:space:]]+)?exec|kubectl[[:space:]]+exec|nomad[[:space:]]+alloc[[:space:]]+exec|pct[[:space:]]+exec|lxc[[:space:]]+exec|podman[[:space:]]+exec|(sh|bash|zsh|dash)[[:space:]]+-c|watch)[^|]*[[:space:]'"'"'"(`](((/[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"'`$()<>]*/)?(ps[[:blank:]]+([a-zA-Z]+([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|[0-9]+([^[:alnum:]_.-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*[fF]|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*[oO]|--format)[[:blank:]=]*[^[:blank:]|;&]*(args|cmd|command)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*O|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*x[a-zA-Z]*([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*u[[:blank:]]*($|[|;&)<>]))|pgrep[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--list-full)|pstree[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--arguments)|top[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*c)))'
_SG_PS_DOCKER='(docker|podman)[[:space:]]+((compose|container)[[:space:]]+)?top([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|(docker|podman)[[:space:]]+(container[[:space:]]+)?(ps|ls)[[:space:]]+([^|;&]*[[:space:]])?--no-trunc'
_SG_PROCTAB_ALL="(${_SG_PROCTAB}|${_SG_PS_REMOTE_T}|${_SG_PS_DOCKER})"
# W11 /proc: the pid position of /proc/<pid>/environ|cmdline -- self, thread-self, digits, $$ $!, a glob token holding * or ?, $name, ${...}, $(...). A placeholder
# word (/proc/<pid>/cmdline, /proc/[pid]/environ) is prose, not a pid.
_SG_PIDTOK='(self|[0-9]+|[^/[:space:]]*[*?][^/[:space:]]*|\$\{[^}]*\}|\$[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*|\$\([^)]*\)|thread-self|\$[$!])'

  # W8 / W11 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): the same tightening for the Forgejo / Gitea app.ini name group and for the ps family of the process-table rows --
  # `ps -ef | grep -v grep` and `cat .../app.ini | grep '^JWT_SECRET'` still print whole command lines / the credential line, so a row-level filter is no credit.
  # wc / hash / discard / name-only credits stay.
  if [[ "$seg" =~ ($_SG_CLAUDE_CFG|$_SG_SRVCFG) ]] || [[ "$seg" =~ $_SG_PROCTAB_ALL ]]; then
  # W12 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): anchored metadata-only forms -- the program is quoted, may start with ONE simple dotted path plus `|`, then exactly
  # one token (keys_unsorted | map_values(length)), and the closing quote follows the token at once. Deliberately NOT added to the word list above: that list
  # accepts anything after `word |`, so `jq 'keys_unsorted | $ENV'` would pass. No tight gate: the form is valid under the claude-config tightening too.
  if _sg_line_match "\|[[:space:]]*jq([[:space:]]+(-[a-zA-Z]+|--[a-z-]+))*[[:space:]]+[\"'](\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*[[:space:]]*\|[[:space:]]*)?(keys_unsorted|map_values\(length\))[\"'][[:space:]]*(\$|\|)" "$seg"; then
    has_filter=1
  fi
# ── secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths (W9 / W10 / W13) ──
# Every function that _sg_judge_one and _sg_per_segment_eval call, directly or through another helper (_sg_core_eval, _sg_emit_blocked, _sg_vx_*, _sg_ext_* and their
# helpers), is defined HERE, above the unit-test sourcing gate: the SC-8 latency gate of the test suite sources this part and then pulls the two functions out by their
# anchors; a helper below the gate is command-not-found (rc=127) in that harness and the gate would stay green on a no-op (W14). The two anchored names and the closing
# `}` on a line of its own must not change. _sg_redact_echo (below the gate since the baseline) only feeds echo text that is discarded in that harness.

# W10 -- path through a shell variable (partial coverage): a SECOND pass that runs only after EVERY built-in verdict allowed (_sg_vx_pass), so the built-in verdict is never
# postponed or changed and the pass can only add blocks. Literal assignments are collected from the whole command (NAME=value, `for NAME in list`, one level of chaining; at
# most 16 assignments and 8 replacements per variable); every segment (the whole command when it degraded) that mentions $NAME or ${NAME} is judged once more with the
# values substituted in. TEXT ONLY: prefix + suffix concatenation with the remainder taken BY LENGTH (${s:N}); no eval (a value may hold a command substitution or back
# quotes: they must never run inside the hook), no ${s//p/r} (bash 5.2 patsub_replacement and the quote semantics of bash < 4.3 disagree about quotes and `&` in the
# replacement), no ${s#*"$pat"} (O(n^2) when the hit sits late in a long string).
# LENGTH BUDGET (review M6; fail-CLOSED, owner ruling 2026-10-14): without it the judged string is the segment plus references x value length, and one level of chaining
# multiplies a value by 8, so a 5 KB command overran the 5 s hook timeout (and a timeout lets the command through). Three limits, counted in characters. Beyond ANY of them the
# whole command is BLOCKED with a text of its own ("command too large to scan for secrets ... split it": _sg_emit_blocked with the 4th word oversize, status 2) and never
# passed: a copy that is not judged is a way past the guard, and padding a value or a chain would be the recipe. The oversize string is never built, expanded further, judged
# or scanned: the pass stops at the first overrun and nothing behind it is touched, so an overrun costs no more than the budgeted work in front of it (blocking is as cheap as
# passing was; the 5 s timeout and its fail-open, 10CG/aria-plugin#216, are not touched). (The COUNTS above -- 16 assignments, 8 replacements, 8 for statements -- are another
# bound and still pass beyond the limit, SC-21 21y.) (1) _SGV_VMAX: a value longer than that (as collected, or after chaining) blocks the
# command whether or not the variable is mentioned: _sg_vx_collect stops at it and returns 2; 4096 is PATH_MAX, a longer value is not one path. (2) _SGV_SMAX: a substituted
# copy (a segment, or the whole command) that would become longer blocks the command (status 2 of _sg_vx_apply; the W9 extension's look at the substituted whole command, after
# the pass, blocks on it only when an extension is in effect -- without one nothing is left unjudged). 8192 because a BLOCK verdict costs the square of the copy length
# (_sg_compute_credit on one long line: about 0.9 s at 8 KB, 3 s at 16 KB, 13 s at 32 KB) and a verdict that arrives after the timeout is lost. The limit counts the whole judged
# string, not its growth: a segment (in whole-command mode: the command) over 8192 characters that mentions a collected variable is blocked however small the value is.
# (3) _SGV_TMAX: all copies of one command together; once used up the command is blocked -- what many dense segments can cost is bounded too.
# _sg_vx_collect, _sg_vx_apply and _SG_SUB are names of the contract, like _sg_vx_pass: the budget probes of the suite use them.
_SGV_N=(); _SGV_V=()
_SG_SUB=""
_SG_RP=""
_SGV_VMAX=4096
_SGV_SMAX=8192
_SGV_TMAX=65536
# _sg_vx_replace S PAT REP [MAX] : literal replace-all of PAT in S, at most 8 replacements -> _SG_RP; with MAX (characters) it stops with status 1 as soon as the result would be
# longer than MAX (_SG_RP is then S unchanged)
_sg_vx_replace() {
  local s="$1" pat="$2" rep="$3" max="${4:-0}" out="" g=0 pre
  _SG_RP="$s"
  while (( g < 8 )) && [[ "$s" == *"$pat"* ]]; do
    g=$((g+1)); pre="${s%%"$pat"*}"; out="${out}${pre}${rep}"; s="${s:$(( ${#pre} + ${#pat} ))}"
    (( max > 0 && ${#out} + ${#s} > max )) && return 1
  done
  _SG_RP="${out}${s}"
}
# _sg_vx_collect S : fills _SGV_N / _SGV_V from S. Status 0 = collected, 2 = a value is longer than _SGV_VMAX (as collected, or after one level of chaining): it stops at that
# value at once -- the value is neither stored nor looked at again and nothing behind it is collected -- and the caller blocks the command.
_sg_vx_collect() {
  local rest="$1" m name val i j n=0 dup pre
  local _q="'" _d='"' _b='`'
  _SGV_N=(); _SGV_V=()
  [[ "$rest" == *'$'* && "$rest" == *=* ]] || [[ "$rest" == *'$'* && "$rest" == *' in '* ]] || return 0
  local re="(^|[;&|[:space:](${_d}${_b}${_q}])((export|declare|local|readonly|typeset)[[:space:]]+(-[a-zA-Z]+[[:space:]]+)*)?([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)=(${_d}([^${_d}]*)${_d}|${_q}([^${_q}]*)${_q}|\\\$\\([^)]*\\)|${_b}[^${_b}]*${_b}|[^[:space:];&|)${_d}${_b}${_q}]*)"
  while (( n < 16 )) && [[ "$rest" =~ $re ]]; do
    (( n += 1 ))
    m="${BASH_REMATCH[0]}"; name="${BASH_REMATCH[5]}"; val="${BASH_REMATCH[6]}"
    pre="${rest%%"$m"*}"; rest="${rest:$(( ${#pre} + ${#m} ))}"
    [[ "$val" == \"*\" && ${#val} -ge 2 ]] && val="${val:1:${#val}-2}"
    [[ "$val" == \'*\' && ${#val} -ge 2 ]] && val="${val:1:${#val}-2}"
    (( ${#val} > _SGV_VMAX )) && return 2
    [[ -z "$val" || "$val" == *"\$$name"* || "$val" == *"\${$name}"* ]] && continue
    dup=0
    for (( i = 0; i < ${#_SGV_N[@]}; i++ )); do
      [[ "${_SGV_N[i]}" == "$name" && "${_SGV_V[i]}" == "$val" ]] && dup=1
    done
    (( dup )) || { _SGV_N+=("$name"); _SGV_V+=("$val"); }
  done
  rest="$1"; n=0
  local rf="(^|[;&|[:space:](])for[[:space:]]+([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)[[:space:]]+in[[:space:]]+([^;&|)]*)"
  while (( n < 8 )) && [[ "$rest" =~ $rf ]]; do
    (( n += 1 ))
    m="${BASH_REMATCH[0]}"; name="${BASH_REMATCH[2]}"; val="${BASH_REMATCH[3]}"
    pre="${rest%%"$m"*}"; rest="${rest:$(( ${#pre} + ${#m} ))}"
    (( ${#val} > _SGV_VMAX )) && return 2
    [[ -z "$val" ]] && continue
    _SGV_N+=("$name"); _SGV_V+=("$val")
  done
  for (( i = 0; i < ${#_SGV_N[@]}; i++ )); do
    for (( j = 0; j < ${#_SGV_N[@]}; j++ )); do
      (( i == j )) && continue
      [[ -z "${_SGV_V[j]}" ]] && continue
      if _sg_vx_replace "${_SGV_V[i]}" "\${${_SGV_N[j]}}" "${_SGV_V[j]}" "$_SGV_VMAX"; then _SGV_V[i]="$_SG_RP"; else return 2; fi
      if _sg_vx_replace "${_SGV_V[i]}" "\$${_SGV_N[j]}" "${_SGV_V[j]}" "$_SGV_VMAX"; then _SGV_V[i]="$_SG_RP"; else return 2; fi
    done
  done
  return 0
}
# _sg_vx_apply S : S with the collected values substituted -> _SG_SUB. Status 0 = a copy was built, 1 = nothing to substitute, 2 = the copy would be longer than _SGV_SMAX (not
# built, _SG_SUB stays S; the caller blocks the command). Per variable: ${NAME} through _sg_vx_replace, then $NAME with the same prefix accumulation (the already substituted text is never scanned again;
# the remainder keeps the character that ended the match, so $f$f works), at most 8 times.
_sg_vx_apply() {
  local s="$1" i n v m tail pre out guard changed=1
  _SG_SUB="$s"
  (( ${#_SGV_N[@]} )) || return 1
  [[ "$s" == *'$'* ]] || return 1
  for (( i = 0; i < ${#_SGV_N[@]}; i++ )); do
    n="${_SGV_N[i]}"; v="${_SGV_V[i]}"
    [[ -n "$v" ]] || continue
    if [[ "$s" == *"\${$n}"* ]]; then _sg_vx_replace "$s" "\${$n}" "$v" "$_SGV_SMAX" || return 2; s="$_SG_RP"; changed=0; fi
    out=""; guard=0
    while (( guard < 8 )) && [[ "$s" =~ \$${n}([^A-Za-z0-9_]|$) ]]; do
      (( guard += 1 ))
      m="${BASH_REMATCH[0]}"; tail="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
      if [[ -z "$tail" ]]; then pre="${s%"$m"}"; else pre="${s%%"$m"*}"; fi
      s="${s:$(( ${#pre} + ${#m} - ${#tail} ))}"
      [[ "$pre" == *\\ ]] && pre="${pre%\\}"
      out="${out}${pre}${v}"; changed=0
      (( ${#out} + ${#s} > _SGV_SMAX )) && return 2
    done
    (( guard )) && s="${out}${s}"
  done
  _SG_SUB="$s"
  return $changed
}
# _sg_vx_pass CMD MODE : the second pass -- judge the substituted copies. MODE is whole or segment, as _sg_core_eval left it in _SG_MODE (in segment mode _SG_SEGS holds
# the segments). The function name is a contract: the fault-injection and evaluation-order probes locate it by name. An overrun of the length budget (a value, a copy, the total)
# ends the pass at once with an oversize BLOCK (the size text of _sg_emit_blocked, status 2): nothing behind the overrun is built or judged.
_sg_vx_pass() {
  local cmd="$1" mode="$2" seg rc ar cr spent=0
  _sg_vx_collect "$cmd"
  cr=$?
  if [[ $cr -eq 2 ]]; then
    _sg_emit_blocked "a variable value is longer than $_SGV_VMAX characters" "$cmd" "$mode" oversize
    return 2
  fi
  (( ${#_SGV_N[@]} )) || return 0
  if [[ "$mode" == whole ]]; then
    _sg_vx_apply "$cmd"
    ar=$?
    if [[ $ar -eq 2 ]]; then
      _sg_emit_blocked "the command is longer than $_SGV_SMAX characters after variable expansion" "$cmd" whole oversize
      return 2
    fi
    if [[ $ar -eq 0 ]]; then _sg_judge_one "$_SG_SUB" whole; return $?; fi
    return 0
  fi
  for seg in "${_SG_SEGS[@]}"; do
    _sg_vx_apply "$seg"
    ar=$?
    [[ $ar -eq 1 ]] && continue
    if [[ $ar -eq 2 ]]; then
      _sg_emit_blocked "a command segment is longer than $_SGV_SMAX characters after variable expansion" "$cmd" segment oversize
      return 2
    fi
    spent=$(( spent + ${#_SG_SUB} ))
    if (( spent > _SGV_TMAX )); then
      _sg_emit_blocked "the command segments together are longer than $_SGV_TMAX characters after variable expansion" "$cmd" segment oversize
      return 2
    fi
    _sg_judge_one "$_SG_SUB" segment
    rc=$?
    [[ $rc -ne 0 ]] && return "$rc"
  done
  return 0
}

# W9 -- project-level extension <project root>/.aria/secret-guard.paths: one LITERAL substring per line; it can only ADD blocks (the built-in verdict always comes first).
# ALL extension code lives in _sg_ext_* functions that are called ONLY through command substitution $( ... ): an internal error (set -u, exit, command not found) kills
# that subshell alone, and the caller treats every output other than the literal sentinel BLOCK as "extension not in effect". The built-in verdict and its fail-closed
# path are never touched (10CG/Aria#154 session-deadlock lesson). Entries and the command text are data: sliced, concatenated and compared as fixed strings, never
# evaluated, expanded or used as a pattern.
_sg_ext_root() {   # prints the project root (CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, else stdin cwd) or nothing
  local r="${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-}"
  if [[ -z "$r" && "$input" == *'"cwd"'* ]]; then
    r="$(printf '%s' "$input" | jq -r '.cwd // ""' 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')"
  fi
  printf '%s' "$r"
}
# _sg_ext_entries : the valid entries of the extension file, lower-cased, one per line; prints nothing when the extension is not in effect (no project root, not a
# readable regular file, > 32 KiB, a NUL byte, no valid entry, > 200 valid entries). An entry has 4..200 BYTES and holds an ASCII letter / digit or a non-ASCII byte;
# comment lines, blank lines, CRs, a leading UTF-8 BOM and surrounding blanks are dropped. Byte semantics: LC_ALL=C is exported for the readers, so lengths and the
# case folding (ASCII letters only) do not depend on the caller's locale; sed only (mawk counts bytes, gawk counts characters under UTF-8).
_sg_ext_entries() {   # prints the valid entries (lower-cased, one per line); prints nothing when the extension is not in effect
  local root f size
  local -x LC_ALL=C
  root="$(_sg_ext_root)"
  [[ -n "$root" ]] || return 0
  f="$root/.aria/secret-guard.paths"
  [[ -f "$f" && -r "$f" ]] || return 0
  size="$(wc -c < "$f" | tr -d ' ')"
  (( size <= 32768 )) || return 0
  tr -d '\000' < "$f" | cmp -s - "$f" || return 0
  local out
  out="$(tr -d '\r' < "$f" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]' | sed -E -e $'1s/^\xef\xbb\xbf//' -e 's/^[[:space:]]+//' -e 's/[[:space:]]+$//' -e '/^#/d' -e '/^.{4,200}$/!d' -e $'/[a-z0-9\x80-\xff]/!d')"
  [[ -n "$out" ]] || return 0
  local n
  n="$(printf '%s\n' "$out" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
  if (( n > 200 )); then   # more than 200 valid entries: the whole file is ignored, not truncated
    return 0
  fi
  printf '%s\n' "$out"
}
# _sg_ext_subst TEXT : TEXT with the first 64 occurrences of any entry (fixed strings, one grep pass, case-insensitive) replaced by the built-in app.ini name;
# prints nothing when no entry occurs.  Byte semantics: `grep -b` gives BYTE offsets, so the slicing counts bytes too (LC_ALL=C, exported).
_sg_ext_subst() {
  local text="$1" hits line off match out="" pos=0 n=0
  local -x LC_ALL=C
  hits="$(grep -oFib -f <(_sg_ext_entries) <(printf '%s' "$text") 2>/dev/null)"
  [[ -n "$hits" ]] || return 0
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    n=$((n+1)); (( n > 64 )) && break
    off="${line%%:*}"; match="${line#*:}"
    out="${out}${text:pos:off-pos}/etc/gitea/app.ini "
    pos=$(( off + ${#match} ))
  done <<< "$hits"
  printf '%s' "${out}${text:pos}"
}
# _sg_ext_pass TEXT : prints the sentinel BLOCK iff the BUILT-IN judgement of the substituted copy (_sg_ext_subst) blocks, nothing otherwise (also when no entry occurs).
# The judgement is _sg_core_eval itself -- _sg_safe_to_split / _sg_split_top / _sg_judge_one, tight credit by the app.ini name -- not a second state machine, so the
# extension agrees with the built-in app.ini rows on every command shape by construction. Its stdout / stderr (the BLOCKED text) are discarded here; the caller prints it.
_sg_ext_pass() {
  local sub
  sub="$(_sg_ext_subst "$1")"
  [[ -n "$sub" ]] || return 0
  _sg_core_eval "$sub" >/dev/null 2>&1
  [[ $? -eq 2 ]] && printf 'BLOCK'
  return 0
}

_sg_ext_path_pass() {   # Read/Edit face: entry (lower-cased) as a literal substring of the path (grep -F -i, one pass)
  local p="$1"
  local -x LC_ALL=C
  if grep -qFi -f <(_sg_ext_entries) <(printf '%s' "$p") 2>/dev/null; then printf 'BLOCK'; fi
  return 0
}

# _sg_emit_blocked PAT SEG MODE [oversize] : the (unchanged) BLOCKED text, shared by every block path. With the 4th word `oversize` it prints the SIZE text instead (W10 length
# budget, fail-closed): nothing was matched, the command is too large to be checked; PAT then names the limit that was hit, SEG and MODE are not used. The size text echoes only
# the first 200 characters of the command (the command is by definition a large one).
_sg_emit_blocked() {
  local pat="$1" seg="$2" mode="$3" kind="${4:-}"
  if [[ "$kind" == oversize ]]; then
    cat >&2 <<EOF
[secret-guard] BLOCKED: command too large to scan for secrets. Limit hit: $pat

Nothing was matched. After variable expansion the command is bigger than the guard
can check reliably, and a command that cannot be checked is blocked (fail-closed),
not allowed.

Split it into smaller commands and run them one at a time:
  - keep every variable value, and every command after expansion, short;
  - do not repeat one long \$VAR many times in a single command;
  - write long files with the Write / Edit tool instead of a heredoc.
命令过大, 无法扫描其中是否含密钥, 请拆成更小的命令

Command was (first 200 characters): $(_sg_redact_echo "${command:0:200}")
EOF
    return 0
  fi
  cat >&2 <<EOF
[secret-guard] BLOCKED: command reads a secret-bearing source without a
value-REDACTING filter. Matched pattern: $pat

Why: raw secrets reaching Claude tool output are sent to Anthropic
servers + prompt-cached. Treat as public leak per CLAUDE.md "NEVER expose
secrets" rule + 2026-05-16 incident.

Acceptable filters (must REDACT, not just pass through):
  | jq 'keys'                            # field NAMES only, no values
  | jq '{safe_alias: .nested.safe_field}' # explicit safe-field allowlist
  | grep '^SAFE_PREFIX='                  # specific safe lines
  | wc -c                                 # count only
  | sha256sum                             # hash only
  >/dev/null   (NOT 2>/dev/null)          # stdout discard
  -o /dev/null                            # curl discard

NOT acceptable (Round 1/2 audit found these create silent bypasses):
  | jq .  / jq -r .  / jq --raw-output .  # identity, returns full input
  | cat / cat -n / tr / head / tail / fold # preserve content
  > /tmp/x.json   # writes file, can be cat'd later
  2> /dev/null    # only stderr discarded, stdout still flows
  | jq '.Items'   # returns full object — use 'keys' to list field names

Reviewed one-off bypass (logged to ~/.claude/logs/guard-bypass.log):
  ... # guard:ack: <reason ≥ 8 NON-WHITESPACE chars describing why>

Command was: $(_sg_redact_echo "$command")
EOF
  if [[ "$mode" == "segment" ]]; then
    echo "Triggering segment: $(_sg_redact_echo "$seg")" >&2
  fi
}

# _sg_core_eval CMD : THE built-in judgement -- the baseline body of _sg_per_segment_eval, unchanged (safe-to-split check, top-level split, one _sg_judge_one per segment);
# it only records how it judged (_SG_MODE) so that the W10 pass knows whether _SG_SEGS holds segments.  The W9 extension calls this same function.
_SG_MODE=segment
_sg_core_eval() {
  local cmd="$1" seg rc
  if ! _sg_safe_to_split "$cmd"; then
    _SG_MODE=whole
    _sg_judge_one "$cmd" whole
    return $?
  fi
  _SG_MODE=segment
  _sg_split_top "$cmd"
  if [[ ${#_SG_SEGS[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then _SG_MODE=none; return 0; fi
  for seg in "${_SG_SEGS[@]}"; do
    _sg_judge_one "$seg" segment
    rc=$?
    [[ $rc -ne 0 ]] && return "$rc"
  done
  return 0
}

    _sg_hit=0
    if echo "$lower_path" | grep -qE '\.env(\.[a-z0-9_.-]+)?$|\.envrc$|/secrets?/|/credentials?/|id_rsa$|id_ed25519$|id_ecdsa$|\.ssh/id_[a-z0-9_]+$|\.pem$|\.key$|\.gpg$|\.age$|\.p12$|\.pfx$|\.jks$|\.tfstate$|\.tfstate\.backup$|/\.aws/credentials$|/\.aws/config$|/\.kube/config$|kubeconfig$|/\.docker/config\.json$|service[_-]account.*\.json$|gcp[_-]key.*\.json$|firebase.*\.json$|\.ssh/known_hosts$|/secret[_-]token|/master[_-]key|/encryption[_-]key|/\.claude/+(\./+)*settings\.json$|/\.claude/+(\./+)*settings\.local\.json$|/\.claude\.json$|[a-z0-9._-]*(forgejo|gitea)[^ ]*app\.ini([^a-z0-9_]|$)|custom/conf/app\.ini([^a-z0-9_]|$)'; then
      # (last two branches: W8 secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths — Forgejo / Gitea app.ini, the lower-cased spelling of $_SG_SRVCFG with the same right
      #  boundary; a Read / Edit of it is blocked, the ack nonce path below stays the escape hatch)
      _sg_hit=1
    fi
    # W9: the project-level extension adds literal-substring blocks on this face too (one grep pass over the path; ASCII case folding). Called ONLY through $( ): this
    # dispatch runs in the main shell, outside the fail-closed subshell, so a bare call that dies would end the hook with exit 1 = allow, built-in blocks included.
    if [[ $_sg_hit -eq 0 ]]; then
      _sgx="$( _sg_ext_path_pass "$file_path" 2>/dev/null )"
      [[ "$_sgx" == BLOCK ]] && _sg_hit=1
    fi
    if [[ $_sg_hit -eq 1 ]]; then
  # W8 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): Forgejo / Gitea app.ini. The 29 printing readers (one by one in SC-16 16q) with their OWN left boundary -- the baseline reader
  # groups have none (`chmod` holds `od`) -- and the NAME front whitelist of Aria #179 before the path; right boundary: no letter / digit / `_` after app.ini.
  # Tight credit (_sg_compute_credit). python3 -c / node -e: see the two source groups below.
  "(^|[^[:alnum:]_.-])(${_SG_READERS_PRINT})[[:space:]]+(([^|]*${_SG_PP_NAME})?(${_SG_SRVCFG}))([^[:alnum:]_]|$)"

  # W8 / W11 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): + the Forgejo / Gitea app.ini name group and the /proc/<pid>/environ|cmdline names (same pid position as the /proc rows).
  "python3?[[:space:]]+-c([^|]*${_SG_PP_SUFFIX})?(/v1/var/|secretsmanager|/secrets/|\.env|provider_key|${_SG_CLAUDE_CFG}|${_SG_SRVCFG}|/proc/${_SG_PIDTOK}/(task/[^/[:space:]]+/)?(environ|cmdline))"
  "node[[:space:]]+-e([^|]*${_SG_PP_SUFFIX})?(/v1/var/|secretsmanager|/secrets/|\.env|provider_key|${_SG_CLAUDE_CFG}|${_SG_SRVCFG}|/proc/${_SG_PIDTOK}/(task/[^/[:space:]]+/)?(environ|cmdline))"
  # W11 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): the two rows above are rewritten IN PLACE. Baseline part unchanged (13 readers; (self|digits); environ|status|cmdline). Added: for
  # environ|cmdline ONLY, 11 more readers (xargs sed grep rg cut paste nl sort base64 cp dd) and the wider pid position _SG_PIDTOK (glob tokens, $$, $name, ${...}, $(...)),
  # plus the task/<tid>/ sub-path. `status` stays on the narrow baseline part (a metadata read such as grep VmRSS /proc/N/status stays allowed). Not a tight family.
  "((cat|head|tail|less|more|strings|hexdump|od|xxd|tr|awk|perl|rev)[[:space:]]+[^|]*/proc/(self|[0-9]+)/(environ|status|cmdline)|(cat|head|tail|less|more|strings|hexdump|od|xxd|tr|awk|perl|rev|xargs|sed|grep|rg|cut|paste|nl|sort|base64|cp|dd)[[:space:]]+[^|]*/proc/${_SG_PIDTOK}/(task/[^/[:space:]]+/)?(environ|cmdline))"
  "(<[[:space:]]*/proc/(self|[0-9]+)/(environ|status|cmdline)|<[[:space:]]*/proc/${_SG_PIDTOK}/(task/[^/[:space:]]+/)?(environ|cmdline))"
  # W11 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): process-table enumeration, three rows spelled as literals (see _SG_PROCTAB for the text and the credit copy).
  # 1. ps / pgrep / pstree / top in an argv-exposing form at a command position, optionally behind one launcher (15 words) and an absolute directory prefix;
  # 2. the same, and whole-environment dumps (env / printenv), behind remote / shell wrappers (ssh, docker|podman|kubectl|lxc exec, nomad alloc exec, pct exec,
  #    sh|bash -c, watch); 3. docker top and docker ps --no-trunc (podman alike). Reject (exit 2) with the shared BLOCKED text.
  '(^|[;&|(){]|`|[[:cntrl:]])[[:blank:]]*((then|do|else|elif)[[:space:]]+)?((/[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"'`$()<>]*/)?(sudo|doas|nice|timeout|xargs|nohup|stdbuf|env|time|eval|setsid|ionice|unbuffer|command|exec)([[:space:]]+(-[^[:space:]]+([[:space:]]+[^-[:space:]|;&()<>`"'"'"'][^[:space:]|;&()<>`"'"'"']*)?|[0-9][0-9.]*[smhd]?|[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*=[^[:space:]]*))*[[:space:]]+)?((/[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"'`$()<>]*/)?(ps[[:blank:]]+([a-zA-Z]+([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|[0-9]+([^[:alnum:]_.-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*[fF]|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*[oO]|--format)[[:blank:]=]*[^[:blank:]|;&]*(args|cmd|command)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*O|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*x[a-zA-Z]*([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*u[[:blank:]]*($|[|;&)<>]))|pgrep[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--list-full)|pstree[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--arguments)|top[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*c))'
  '(ssh|docker[[:space:]]+(compose[[:space:]]+)?exec|kubectl[[:space:]]+exec|nomad[[:space:]]+alloc[[:space:]]+exec|pct[[:space:]]+exec|lxc[[:space:]]+exec|podman[[:space:]]+exec|(sh|bash|zsh|dash)[[:space:]]+-c|watch)[^|]*[[:space:]'"'"'"(`](((/[^[:space:]|;&"'"'"'`$()<>]*/)?(ps[[:blank:]]+([a-zA-Z]+([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|[0-9]+([^[:alnum:]_.-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*[fF]|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*[oO]|--format)[[:blank:]=]*[^[:blank:]|;&]*(args|cmd|command)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*O|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*x[a-zA-Z]*([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*u[[:blank:]]*($|[|;&)<>]))|pgrep[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--list-full)|pstree[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?(-[a-zA-Z]*a|--arguments)|top[[:blank:]]+([^|;&]*[[:blank:]])?-[a-zA-Z]*c))|printenv([[:space:]'"'"'"]|[;&|)}<>`]|$)|env([[:blank:]]*($|[;&|)}<>'"'"'"`])))'
  '(docker|podman)[[:space:]]+((compose|container)[[:space:]]+)?top([^[:alnum:]_-]|$)|(docker|podman)[[:space:]]+(container[[:space:]]+)?(ps|ls)[[:space:]]+([^|;&]*[[:space:]])?--no-trunc'
  local credit="" subj="$1" _w
  # W12 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): an os.environ single-key read (.environ.get( / .environb.get( / .environ[ / .environb[) is the same information as the
  # allowed os.getenv, but its `.env` prefix trips the interpreter rows. Every occurrence of the four literal substrings is cut out of the COPY that is
  # matched ($subj) and replaced by a neutral token without `.env`; the whole-table forms (os.environ, dict(os.environ), .items(), ...) are not in the list
  # and stay blocked. Prefix + length-based remainder concatenation, no ${s//p/r} and no eval: the key texts are never executed. credit and the BLOCKED
  # text keep working on the original $seg / $command.
  if [[ "$subj" == *environ* ]]; then
    while [[ "$subj" == *".environ.get("* ]]; do _w="${subj%%".environ.get("*}"; subj="${_w}.ek_(${subj:$(( ${#_w} + 13 ))}"; done
    while [[ "$subj" == *".environb.get("* ]]; do _w="${subj%%".environb.get("*}"; subj="${_w}.ek_(${subj:$(( ${#_w} + 14 ))}"; done
    while [[ "$subj" == *".environ["* ]]; do _w="${subj%%".environ["*}"; subj="${_w}.ek_[${subj:$(( ${#_w} + 9 ))}"; done
    while [[ "$subj" == *".environb["* ]]; do _w="${subj%%".environb["*}"; subj="${_w}.ek_[${subj:$(( ${#_w} + 10 ))}"; done
  fi
    if [[ "$subj" =~ $pat ]]; then
        _sg_emit_blocked "$pat" "$seg" "$mode"
# Order (W9 / W10): 1. the built-in judgement (_sg_core_eval: the baseline body of this function, unchanged); 2. only if it allowed, the W10 variable pass (_sg_vx_pass);
# 3. only if both allowed, the W9 project extension -- on the original command, then on the variable-substituted whole command. A built-in block ends the evaluation
# before either later stage is even called, so nothing the built-ins decide is postponed or reversed. The extension runs ONLY inside command substitutions; its answer
# is the literal sentinel BLOCK, anything else (empty, error, fault) means "no extension block". Its own block prints the shared BLOCKED text (W13). Length budget: an overrun
# inside _sg_vx_pass blocks there; the extension's look at the substituted whole command, when that copy is too large to build, blocks here -- but only when an extension is in
# effect (entries exist: asked inside a command substitution like every other extension call), because without one nothing is left unjudged.
  local cmd="$1" rc ar _x
  _sg_core_eval "$cmd"
  rc=$?
  [[ $rc -ne 0 ]] && return "$rc"
  if [[ "$_SG_MODE" != none ]]; then
    _sg_vx_pass "$cmd" "$_SG_MODE"
  fi
  _x="$( _sg_ext_pass "$cmd" 2>/dev/null )"
  if [[ "$_x" != BLOCK ]] && (( ${#_SGV_N[@]} )); then
    _sg_vx_apply "$cmd"
    ar=$?
    if [[ $ar -eq 0 ]]; then
      _x="$( _sg_ext_pass "$_SG_SUB" 2>/dev/null )"
    elif [[ $ar -eq 2 && -n "$( _sg_ext_entries 2>/dev/null )" ]]; then
      _sg_emit_blocked "the command is longer than $_SGV_SMAX characters after variable expansion, too large for the project extension (.aria/secret-guard.paths) to check" "$cmd" segment oversize
      return 2
    fi
  fi
  if [[ "$_x" == BLOCK ]]; then
    _sg_emit_blocked "(extension) .aria/secret-guard.paths" "$cmd" segment
    return 2
  fi
++ b/hooks/secret-scan.sh
# The alert names the matched tags and the tool the output came from, never any
# text of tool_input; the log line carries an fp= field (one fingerprint per
# counted span, never the value). Scanned: Bash output, a Read result
# (tool_response.file.content), a Write response, and — through the
# PostToolUseFailure event — the error text of a failed call.
#
# the tool result has already been produced, and hook stdout does not substitute
# the captured result (official hooks-guide line 891: "PostToolUse hooks can't
# undo actions since the tool has already executed"). The Claude Code 2.1.285
# schema of the PostToolUse hook output does list updatedToolOutput; that is
# unverified end to end and unused by this hook. Detection here is a mitigation
# (stop Claude from echoing the value + prompt rotation), NOT prevention. The
# real prevention layer is the PreToolUse `secret-guard` hook, which blocks
# risky commands BEFORE they run.
#   PreToolUse:  "Don't run this command"      (effective prevention)
#   PostToolUse: "This output leaked a secret"  (detect + warn only)
#   - bcrypt password hashes
#   - generic key-shape tags (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): json-credential-field,
#     json-env-secret-key, kv-secret-assign (`KEY = value` in INI / env / YAML),
#     auth-header-token (`Authorization: token|Basic`), cf-access-client-secret,
#     cli-secret-flag (`--token=<value>`). A placeholder whitelist (FAKE / PLACEHOLDER /
#     [REDACTED] markers, <...>, ${...}, masks) keeps json-secret-field and these tags
#     silent; an entropy floor, file-path values and dotted identifier chains
#     (process.env.X) are silent for the new tags too.
# - Secrets encoded base64 / hex / etc (without telltale prefix AND without a credential key name)
# - Under the key-shape tags: values shorter than 16 characters or of a single
#   character class, lower-case YAML / INI keys, space-separated CLI arguments
# - Successful Edit / MultiEdit results (their response holds no scanned field)
# Input (stdin JSON; a union of the shapes Claude Code 2.1.285 sends):
#     "hook_event_name": "PostToolUse" | "PostToolUseFailure",
#     "tool_name": "Bash" | "Read" | "Edit" | "Write" | "MultiEdit",
#     "tool_response": {                       // PostToolUse only
#       "stdout": "...",                       // Bash (older versions: "output")
#       "file": {"content": "..."},            // Read: tool_response.file.content
#       "filePath": "...", "content": "..."    // Write: tool_response.content
#     },
#     "error": "..."                           // PostToolUseFailure only: `Exit code N` + stderr + stdout
# A failed call (a Bash command that exits non-zero, ...) arrives as
# PostToolUseFailure with NO tool_response; its text is the top-level error.
#       {"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "PostToolUse" | "PostToolUseFailure",
#         "additionalContext": "[secret-scan] DETECTED N ... (tags: ...; source: <tool>) ..."},
#     hookEventName must name the event that fired: the harness rejects a mismatch.
#
# === History ===
#   v1.24.0: initial PostToolUse detector (detect + warn only).
#   secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths (10CG/aria-plugin#154, #203): a Read result,
#         NUL-delimited bodies and the error text of failed calls
#         (PostToolUseFailure) are scanned; the key-shape tags above, the
#         placeholder whitelist, fp= value fingerprints in the log and
#         tags + source in the alert text were added.
# Event (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths W1): this hook is registered for PostToolUse AND PostToolUseFailure. A failed tool call (a Bash command that exits
# non-zero, ...) does not trigger PostToolUse: it arrives as PostToolUseFailure with NO tool_response and the call's text (`Exit code N` + stderr + stdout) in the
# top-level `error`. hookSpecificOutput.hookEventName must name the event that fired — the harness rejects a mismatch and the alert never reaches the model. A substring
# pre-check keeps the common path free of an extra jq process; jq confirms, so output text that merely MENTIONS the word does not flip the event.
hook_event="PostToolUse"
if [[ "$input" == *PostToolUseFailure* ]]; then
  ev_name="$(printf '%s' "$input" | jq -r '.hook_event_name // ""' 2>/dev/null)"
  ev_name="${ev_name%$'\r'}"   # crlf-strip: comparison value
  [[ "$ev_name" == "PostToolUseFailure" ]] && hook_event="PostToolUseFailure"
fi

# Bash: tool_response.stdout (older: .output); Read: tool_response.file.content (older: .content); Write: tool_response.content;
# a failed call (PostToolUseFailure): .error. Try multiple field names since Claude Code versions vary.
# The filter is kept in a variable so that the NUL-delimited second view below runs the very same program.
_content_prog='  # crlf-ok: data body being SCANNED (internal counting scratch only) — must NOT CR-strip (would corrupt user content, Spec C2)
  .tool_response.file.content //
  .error //
'
content="$(printf '%s' "$input" | jq -r "$_content_prog" 2>/dev/null)"   # crlf-ok: data body being SCANNED (internal counting scratch only) — must NOT CR-strip

# NUL bytes (W1). Bash drops them inside a command substitution: that is the view above, and why text with a NUL after every character (UTF-16LE shown by a tool)
# scans as the ASCII it spells. A NUL-DELIMITED body (/proc/<pid>/environ, `env -0`) is glued into one line by that view, so `KEY=value` records lose their line
# starts. When the raw envelope holds a JSON \u0000 escape (one string comparison; no extra process otherwise) a SECOND view is appended: the same extraction with
# NUL -> newline. It is appended only when it differs from the first, so input without a real NUL is scanned exactly as before (a text that merely MENTIONS the six
# characters \u0000 leaves the two views equal). Any transformation of the input must be a superset of what the baseline view detects — hence a second view, not a replacement.
if [[ "$input" == *'\u0000'* ]]; then
  nul_view="$(printf '%s' "$input" | jq -r "$_content_prog" 2>/dev/null | tr '\000' '\n')"   # crlf-ok: data body being SCANNED (same filter, NUL -> newline)
  [[ -n "$nul_view" && "$nul_view" != "$content" ]] && content="${content}"$'\n'"${nul_view}"
fi
  # ── W2 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): six generic key-shape tags, in THIS order (the order is the fingerprint traversal order, W4). They sit after
  # json-secret-field and before bcrypt-hash; the 31 patterns above keep their regex and their order. Every span they match goes through the classifier below
  # (value -> W3 whitelist -> entropy floor -> path form -> dotted identifier). ERE only (grep -E / sed -E), no backtracking, and LINEAR in the input length: no
  # unbounded greedy prefix in front of a character-class run (kv: key-name affixes of at most 64 characters each, cli: flag-name prefix of at most 40, both with a
  # left boundary; JSON keys start with `"`, header names with a literal). Header names are spelled as per-letter classes: BSD sed has neither -i nor the I modifier.
  # 1. json-credential-field: JSON key from a closed table (17 stems x snake / camel / Pascal / joined spellings = 58 names), string value of 16 or more characters.
  'json-credential-field|"(AccessToken|ApiKey|ApiToken|AuthToken|BearerToken|ClientSecret|JwtSecret|Passwd|Password|PrivateKey|RefreshToken|RegistrationToken|Secret|SecretKey|SessionToken|Sha1|Token|accessToken|access_token|accesstoken|apiKey|apiToken|api_key|api_token|apikey|apitoken|authToken|auth_token|authtoken|bearerToken|bearer_token|bearertoken|clientSecret|client_secret|clientsecret|jwtSecret|jwt_secret|jwtsecret|passwd|password|privateKey|private_key|privatekey|refreshToken|refresh_token|refreshtoken|registrationToken|registration_token|registrationtoken|secret|secretKey|secret_key|secretkey|sessionToken|session_token|sessiontoken|sha1|token)"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"[^"]{16,}"'
  # 2. json-env-secret-key: JSON key that is an upper-case environment variable name holding one of the keywords (Nomad / compose `Env` maps), value of 16 or more.
  'json-env-secret-key|"[A-Z0-9_]*(SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSWD|TOKEN|API_KEY|APIKEY|PRIVATE_KEY|WEBHOOK|ENCRYPTION_KEY|ACCESS_KEY)[A-Z0-9_]*"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*"[^"]{16,}"'
  # 3. kv-secret-assign: INI / env / YAML assignment; upper-case key holding a keyword, optional `export`, `=` or `:` with optional blanks, one optional quote layer.
  'kv-secret-assign|(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_])(export[[:space:]]+)?[A-Z0-9_]{0,64}(SECRET|PASSWORD|PASSWD|TOKEN|API_KEY|APIKEY|PRIVATE_KEY|WEBHOOK|ENCRYPTION_KEY|ACCESS_KEY)[A-Z0-9_]{0,64}[[:space:]]*[=:][[:space:]]*["'"'"']?[A-Za-z0-9+/=._~-]{16,}'
  # 4. auth-header-token: `Authorization: token <v>` / `Authorization: Basic <v>` (header name case-insensitive: curl -v under HTTP/2 echoes it in lower case).
  'auth-header-token|[aA][uU][tT][hH][oO][rR][iI][zZ][aA][tT][iI][oO][nN]:[[:space:]]*([Tt]oken|[Bb]asic)[[:space:]]+[A-Za-z0-9._/+=-]{16,}'
  # 5. cf-access-client-secret: `CF-Access-Client-Secret: <v>` (header name case-insensitive).
  'cf-access-client-secret|[cC][fF]-[aA][cC][cC][eE][sS][sS]-[cC][lL][iI][eE][nN][tT]-[sS][eE][cC][rR][eE][tT]:[[:space:]]*[A-Za-z0-9._/+=-]{16,}'
  # 6. cli-secret-flag: `--token=<v>` style flags (`-` or `--`, a name ending in a keyword, the value joined with `=`).
  'cli-secret-flag|(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])--?[A-Za-z0-9-]{0,40}(token|password|passwd|secret|api-key|apikey|access-key)=[A-Za-z0-9+/=._~-]{16,}'

# ── W2 classifier / W3 whitelist / W4 fingerprint helpers ──────────────────
# (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths.) A span that a pattern matched is handled here BEFORE the counting substitution consumes it: the credential body is cut out of
# the span (_value_of), the span is filtered, and what still counts is fingerprinted (W4). Filtering: the W3 whitelist for the six W2 tags and json-secret-field;
# entropy floor, path form and dotted identifier for the six W2 tags only. No other tag is filtered: their detections stay those of the baseline.
# Spans are UNTRUSTED text: the helpers only slice strings and use [[ =~ ]] -- no eval / source, no command substitution or arithmetic on the content. The
# classification path has no fork (builtins and [[ =~ ]]); helpers hand their result back through the global V. Regexes live in variables: bash 3.2 cannot parse
# a bare `<` inside [[ ]].
CLASSIFIED_TAGS=" json-secret-field json-credential-field json-env-secret-key kv-secret-assign auth-header-token cf-access-client-secret cli-secret-flag "
NEW_TAGS=" json-credential-field json-env-secret-key kv-secret-assign auth-header-token cf-access-client-secret cli-secret-flag "
V=""
# Character classes are ENUMERATED, never written as ranges: in bash < 5 under a non-C locale a glob range such as [a-z] follows the collation order (real bash
# 3.2.57 with en_US.UTF-8: [[ QWERTY == *[a-z]* ]] is true), which would let the entropy floor count capitals as lower case. (The ERE patterns above keep the
# baseline's range spelling: grep / sed read them by code point.)
_AZ='ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ'; _az='abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz'; _09='0123456789'
_RE_TRIM='^[[:space:]]*(.*[^[:space:]])[[:space:]]*$'
_RE_LASTWORD='([^[:space:]]+)$'
_RE_PFX_COLON='^[^:]*:'                                              # up to and including the first `:`
_RE_PFX_EQ='^[^=]*='                                                 # up to and including the first `=`
_RE_PFX_KV="^[^${_AZ}${_09}_]*[${_AZ}${_09}_]*[[:space:]]*[=:]"      # kv-secret-assign: boundary character / `export` / blanks, the key, blanks, `=` or `:`
_RE_URL_PW='^[^:]*://[^:]*:([^@]*)@'                                 # scheme://user:PASSWORD@  (the password: behind the first `:` after the user, up to the first `@`)
# Value extraction is LINEAR in the span length. Every `#` / `##` / `%` / `%%` deletion in this code finds a delimiter that is certainly there and lies within a
# constant number of characters of the start (the `:` behind a fixed header name, the quotes round a JSON value, the `://` behind the scheme). A delimiter that can
# be far away -- behind a blank run, a long key, a long password -- is found by ONE [[ =~ ]] scan and the rest is cut by LENGTH (${s:N}); the one optional character
# left (a quote) is tested with ${V:0:1}. NOT ${V#["']} or ${s%%X*} where the delimiter may be missing or far: bash tries the positions one by one and each try
# costs O(length), so a 300 KB value takes seconds of CPU, past the hook's 5 s timeout (SC-34).
_trimV() { if [[ "$V" =~ $_RE_TRIM ]]; then V="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"; else V=""; fi; }
_cut_pfx() {   # $1 regex  $2 span  -> V = the span after the regex's match (one scan finds it, ${s:N} cuts); no match -> the span itself (counted, never dropped)
  if [[ "$2" =~ $1 ]]; then V="${2:${#BASH_REMATCH[0]}}"; else V="$2"; fi
}
_value_of() {   # $1 tag  $2 span  -> V = the credential body (the W4 table): what W3 / W2 classify and what is fingerprinted
  local tag="$1" span="$2" k
  case "$tag" in
    json-secret-field|json-credential-field|json-env-secret-key)
      _cut_pfx "$_RE_PFX_COLON" "$span"; _trimV; V="${V#\"}"; V="${V%\"}" ;;
    kv-secret-assign)
      _cut_pfx "$_RE_PFX_KV" "$span"; _trimV; case "${V:0:1}" in \"|\') V="${V:1}" ;; esac ;;
    auth-header-token|bearer-token) if [[ "$span" =~ $_RE_LASTWORD ]]; then V="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"; else V="$span"; fi ;;
    cf-access-client-secret|x-api-key-header) k="${span%%:*}"; V="${span:${#k}+1}"; _trimV ;;
    cli-secret-flag|env-line-secret-keyword) _cut_pfx "$_RE_PFX_EQ" "$span" ;;
    postgres-url|redis-url|mongodb-url|basic-auth-url)
      if [[ "$span" =~ $_RE_URL_PW ]]; then V="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"; else V="$span"; fi ;;
    gcp-private-key-id) k="${span%%:*}"; V="${span:${#k}+1}"; _trimV; V="${V#\"}"; V="${V%\"}" ;;
    pem-private-key-block|pem-header-only) V="" ;;
    *) V="$span" ;;   # provider-prefix keys, jwt, webhooks, bcrypt: the whole span is the credential
  esac
}
_RE_RED='^\[REDACTED[^]]*\]$'
_RE_ANGLE='^[<][^<>]*[>]$'
_RE_VARB='^\$\{[^}]*\}$'
_RE_TMPL='^\$?\{\{.*\}\}$'
_RE_MASK='^(\*+|x+|X+)$'
_wl_match() {   # W3 whitelist. $1 value; rc 0 = placeholder (not counted). WHOLE-value placeholder forms and deliberate marker words only, never a one-character prefix
  local v="$1"
  case "$v" in
    FAKE*|PLACEHOLDER*|NOT-REAL*|NOT_REAL*|NOTREAL*|REDACTED*) return 0 ;;   # marker words at the start of the value (case-sensitive)
  esac
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_RED ]] && return 0     # [REDACTED...] with the bracket closed
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_ANGLE ]] && return 0   # <...> without a < or > inside (also the counting sentinel <secret-scan-counted:TAG>)
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_VARB ]] && return 0    # ${...}
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_TMPL ]] && return 0    # {{...}} and ${{...}}
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_MASK ]] && return 0    # only * or x or X, repeated
  [[ "$v" == *"…" ]] && return 0         # ends with U+2026 (a truncated display); the ASCII "..." is NOT included
  return 1
}
_entropy_ok() {   # W2 entropy floor: at least two of the three classes lower / upper / digit (symbols are no class)
  local v="$1" n=0
  [[ "$v" == *[abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz]* ]] && n=$((n+1))
  [[ "$v" == *[ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ]* ]] && n=$((n+1))
  [[ "$v" == *[0123456789]* ]] && n=$((n+1))
  (( n >= 2 ))
}
_RE_PATHLEAD='^(/|~/|\./|\.\./)'
_RE_PATHCH="^[${_AZ}${_az}${_09}._~/-]+\$"
_RE_DOTID="^[${_AZ}${_az}_][${_AZ}${_az}${_09}_]*(\\.[${_AZ}${_az}_][${_AZ}${_az}${_09}_]*)+\$"
_path_form() {   # W2: a file path -- starts with / ~/ ./ ../, only [A-Za-z0-9._~/-], at least two `/` (`+` and `=` are no path characters: a base64 value is not a path)
  local v="$1"
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_PATHLEAD ]] || return 1
  [[ "$v" =~ $_RE_PATHCH ]] || return 1
  [[ "$v" == */*/* ]]
}
_dotted_ident() { [[ "$1" =~ $_RE_DOTID ]]; }   # W2: a dotted identifier chain (process.env.X, settings.X): the value is read from the environment / configuration
FP_ITEMS=""; FP_N=0
_fp_push() {   # $1 item -> the fingerprint list of the log line (W4), at most 10 items
  (( FP_N >= 10 )) && return 0
  FP_ITEMS="${FP_ITEMS:+$FP_ITEMS,}$1"; FP_N=$((FP_N+1))
}
_fp_add() {   # $1 credential body -> first 8 hex digits of its sha256 (value of 16 or more characters), L<length> for a shorter one (no hash: an unsalted prefix would
              # let a dictionary confirm a short password), "-" when neither sha256sum nor shasum can be run (fail-soft: the log line is still written)
  (( FP_N >= 10 )) && return 0
  local v="$1" h="" it
  if (( ${#v} < 16 )); then it="L${#v}"
  else
    if command -v sha256sum >/dev/null 2>&1; then h="$(printf '%s' "$v" | sha256sum 2>/dev/null)"
    elif command -v shasum >/dev/null 2>&1; then h="$(printf '%s' "$v" | shasum -a 256 2>/dev/null)"; fi
    it="${h:0:8}"; it="${it:--}"
  fi
  _fp_push "$it"
}

      pem_i=0   # W4: the PEM pre-pass comes first in the fingerprint order; a PEM block's item is "-" (no value is taken from it)
      while (( pem_i < pem_count && FP_N < 10 )); do _fp_push "-"; pem_i=$(( pem_i + 1 )); done
    _fp_push "-"   # W4: same item as a PEM block
    # W2 / W3 / W4: classification and fingerprints, one pass over the FIRST 200 spans of this tag, BEFORE the counting substitution consumes them (a span that the
    # classifier excludes is consumed all the same: nothing after it should count it). `keep` = the spans that count. The spans beyond 200 are not iterated: no
    # value, no classification, no fingerprint, they are added as (count - 200) -- fail-closed counting with a bounded iteration cost (SC-11 11w).
    fp_mark_items="$FP_ITEMS"; fp_mark_n="$FP_N"
    keep=0
    while IFS= read -r span; do
      _value_of "$tag" "$span"; v="$V"
      if [[ "$CLASSIFIED_TAGS" == *" $tag "* ]]; then
        _wl_match "$v" && continue
        if [[ "$NEW_TAGS" == *" $tag "* ]]; then
          _entropy_ok "$v" || continue
          _path_form "$v" && continue
          _dotted_ident "$v" && continue
        fi
      fi
      keep=$((keep+1))
      _fp_add "$v"
    done < <(grep -oE -- "$pattern" "$tmpfile" 2>/dev/null | head -n 200)
    (( count > 200 )) && keep=$(( keep + count - 200 ))
      FP_ITEMS="$fp_mark_items"; FP_N="$fp_mark_n"   # W4: spans that are not counted are not fingerprinted either
      # Adjust the count to reflect what actually got isolated (keep - residual; keep = count minus the spans W2 / W3 exclude)
      actual_counted=$(( keep - residual ))
    if (( keep > 0 )); then   # every span of this tag may have been excluded by the W2 / W3 filters: then the tag is not reported at all
      matches_total=$(( matches_total + keep ))
      matches_breakdown="${matches_breakdown}${tag}=${keep} "
    fi
# W4: the 9th field `fp=` holds one fingerprint per counted span (at most 10, comma separated; sha256 hex prefix, L<length> or "-", see _fp_add), never a value.
printf '%s\t%s\t%s\tSCAN-DETECT\ttool=%s\tmatches=%s\tbreakdown=%s\tsize=%s\tfp=%s\n' \
  "$tool" "$matches_total" "${matches_breakdown% }" "$input_size" "${FP_ITEMS:--}" \
# W5: descriptive only -- the tag breakdown (the hook's own tag table and counts) and the harness tool name. NEVER any tool_input text (file_path, command): it is
# decided by the repository or conversation being read, and additionalContext is the channel the model trusts most.
addl_msg="[secret-scan] DETECTED ${matches_total} secret-shape match(es) in tool output (tags: ${matches_breakdown% }; source: ${tool}) — treat as already-leaked; do NOT repeat the value(s) in your reply; recommend rotating the affected credential(s). (This hook detects+warns only; it cannot redact — PostToolUse runs after the tool result is already produced.)"
jq -n --arg ev "$hook_event" --arg addl "$addl_msg" --arg sys "$sys_msg" '{
    hookEventName: $ev,
++ b/hooks/tests/secret-guard.test.sh
# Coverage: 822 cases (816 without zsh) across Bash (block/allow), Read/Edit (block/allow),
# W6 (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): the HOME this suite was started with, recorded before anything can change it.
# The hygiene case near the end compares against it to tell whether a hook run of this suite wrote into the caller's real
# ~/.claude/logs (the isolation itself is a separate block that must come AFTER this line).
_wpa_orig_home="${HOME:-}"

# W6 test hygiene (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths): this suite never writes into the caller's real HOME. A guard:ack bypass is logged to $HOME/.claude/logs
# (guard-bypass.log); without this block every run of the suite appended its ack events to the real audit log. The temp dir is removed on exit. Keep the block
# after the _wpa_orig_home line above (the W6 case compares against it) and before the first hook run.
_TEST_HOME="$(mktemp -d)"
case "$_TEST_HOME" in /*) ;; *) _TEST_HOME="$PWD/$_TEST_HOME" ;; esac
if [[ -z "$_TEST_HOME" || ! -d "$_TEST_HOME" ]]; then echo "secret-guard.test.sh: cannot create a private HOME (mktemp -d failed)" >&2; exit 1; fi
HOME="$_TEST_HOME"
export HOME
trap 'rm -rf "$_TEST_HOME"' EXIT

# spanning families. census family_count is asserted ==64: 57 was the #128
# +3 = the W8 app.ini reader row, the W11 /proc row (rewritten in place: its leading group is now a two-branch group, a new key) and the W11 remote / shell
# wrapper row (secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths TASK-005, 2026-10-08, deliberate; each carries its own SC-19 probe below).
if [[ "$sc19_family_count" != "64" ]]; then
  failures+=("FAIL [SC-19: census family_count]: want 64, got $sc19_family_count -- census grouping has drifted from the spec's own baseline; investigate before trusting the per-family check below")
# ── W8 / W11 additions to the SC-19 spanning set (3 new families, secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths TASK-005, 2026-10-08) ──
# Same engineered shape as the probes above: the whole string hits the family's row, every top-level segment individually misses it -- per-segment allows (exit 0) =
# the documented cross-segment fail-open class (#138 tracks the cure). The census evaluates the array WITHOUT the shared name variables, which is why the W8 row's key is
# GRP:() (its reader group is empty there) and the /proc key shows `/proc//`. The /proc probe uses a reader (sed) and a pid token ($$) that only the NEW branch has.
# family='GRP:()' | 2->0 by construction (W8 row is new; no pre-W8 baseline exists)
bash_case "SC-19 fam:srvcfg-reader cross-seg" 0 \
  'cat notes.txt; echo /etc/forgejo/app.ini'
# family='GRP:((cat|head|tail|less|more|strings|hexdump|od|xxd|tr|awk|perl|rev)[[:space:]]+[^|]*/proc/(self|[0-9]+)/(environ|status|cmdline)|(cat|head|tail|less|more|strings|hexdump|od|xxd|tr|awk|perl|rev|xargs|sed|grep|rg|cut|paste|nl|sort|base64|cp|dd)[[:space:]]+[^|]*/proc//(task/[^/[:space:]]+/)?(environ|cmdline))' | 2->0 by construction (the sed / $$ branch is new; the baseline /proc row had the key of the cat|head|... reader group)
bash_case "SC-19 fam:proc-reader-relaxed cross-seg" 0 \
  'sed notes.txt; echo /proc/$$/environ'
# family='GRP:(ssh|docker[[:space:]]+(compose[[:space:]]+)?exec|kubectl[[:space:]]+exec|nomad[[:space:]]+alloc[[:space:]]+exec|pct[[:space:]]+exec|lxc[[:space:]]+exec|podman[[:space:]]+exec|(sh|bash|zsh|dash)[[:space:]]+-c|watch)' | 2->0 by construction (W11 wrapper row is new; no pre-W11 baseline exists)
bash_case "SC-19 fam:ps-wrapper cross-seg" 0 \
  'ssh myhost uptime; echo ps aux'
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths (10CG/aria-plugin#154 · 10CG/aria-plugin#203 · 10CG/Aria#221)
# L1 critical-subset regression: one group per work item W8-W13 plus the W14 coupling checks.
#
# Test-first: this block was written BEFORE the implementation. Case names carry the work item, the SC row
# of the Spec (openspec/changes/secret-net-l3-and-bypass-paths/proposal.md, baseline_probe.py) and a category:
#   bf = baseline-failing  red on the baseline hook (aria 268da8f), green once the work item has landed
#   rg = reverse-guard     holds on the baseline and must keep holding (something that must not move)
#   ag = allow-guard       the command stays allowed on the baseline and after (false-positive guard)
#   kl = known-limit       pins a documented gap as it is today; red = somebody closed it (update the Spec then)
# The acceptance tool for the full 454-row matrix is baseline_probe.py; this block is the part of it that stays
# with the suite as a regression net (owner ruling 2026-10-07: probe = acceptance, suites = key subset).
# Naming: the helpers and globals of this block are prefixed wpa_ / WPA_ (work package A of this change); nothing outside it uses them.
#
# Every command below is handed to the hook as DATA on stdin; nothing here is executed. Rule #7: no credential-shaped
# literal. The hook runs of this block use a private HOME, a scratch working directory and no CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR /
# SECRET_GUARD_ACK_* from the caller, so they neither write the caller's real ~/.claude/logs nor depend on a project's
# .aria/secret-guard.paths (the W6 case is the exception: it runs the hook with the suite's own HOME on purpose).
# Single-command cases that expect a block (exit 2) use the plain bash_case / read_case helpers of this file (an inherited
# project extension could only add blocks to them); everything that expects an allow (exit 0) or needs a project / ACK
# environment goes through the wpa_ helpers, which pin the environment.
# Aggregated cases (wpa_all_* / wpa_begin_bash_case ... wpa_end_case) count once; a failure names the first six
# members that are off. Case names stay unique: the helpers below are named so that the duplicate-name self-check
# (SC-17) covers them, and their bodies pass the name on as "$name", the form that check already leaves out.
# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

wpa_tmp="$(mktemp -d)"
case "$wpa_tmp" in /*) ;; *) wpa_tmp="$PWD/$wpa_tmp" ;; esac     # absolute even when TMPDIR is relative
# Absolute paths, resolved ONCE. $HOOK and $0 come from how the suite was started: when it is started with a relative path (the probe does:
# `bash hooks/tests/<suite>` from the aria root) they are relative, and every helper below that changes directory first would lose the hook.
# So nothing in this block uses $HOOK / $0 / dirname "$0" after a cd; it uses these.
case "$HOOK" in /*) wpa_hook="$HOOK" ;; *) wpa_hook="$PWD/$HOOK" ;; esac     # the hook under test
case "$0" in /*) wpa_self="$0" ;; *) wpa_self="$PWD/$0" ;; esac              # this suite file
wpa_home="$wpa_tmp/home"
wpa_cwd="$wpa_tmp/cwd"
mkdir -p "$wpa_home" "$wpa_cwd/gl"
: > "$wpa_cwd/gl/stxxar.conf"      # the decoy a file-name glob of the entry `gl/st*ar.conf` would pick up (W9 no-execution case)

WPA_HOOK="$wpa_hook"                # the hook under test, ABSOLUTE (wpa_rc / wpa_err cd first); a patched copy for the fault-injection / marker cases
WPA_ENV=()                          # extra `env` words for the hook run: `-u NAME` words first, then VAR=value words; wpa_use_* set it
WPA_CWD=""                          # a stdin `cwd` field, when non-empty
wpa_nl=$'\n'
wpa_use_none() { WPA_ENV=(-u CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR -u SECRET_GUARD_ACK_PATH -u SECRET_GUARD_ACK_NONCE "HOME=$wpa_home"); WPA_CWD=""; WPA_HOOK="$wpa_hook"; }
wpa_use_proj() { WPA_ENV=(-u SECRET_GUARD_ACK_PATH -u SECRET_GUARD_ACK_NONCE "HOME=$wpa_home" "CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=$1"); WPA_CWD=""; WPA_HOOK="$wpa_hook"; }   # <project root>
wpa_lang_only() { WPA_ENV=(-u LC_ALL "${WPA_ENV[@]}" "LANG=C.UTF-8"); }     # the hook's environment holds LANG but no LC_ALL (a container without LC_ALL)
wpa_use_none

# the stdin JSON of one tool call, as Claude Code sends it (plus a `cwd` field when WPA_CWD is set)
wpa_in() {   # <tool> <payload: command | file_path>
  if [[ "$1" == Bash ]]; then
    jq -nc --arg c "$2" --arg w "$WPA_CWD" '{tool_name:"Bash",tool_input:{command:$c}} + (if $w == "" then {} else {cwd:$w} end)'
  else
    jq -nc --arg t "$1" --arg f "$2" --arg w "$WPA_CWD" '{tool_name:$t,tool_input:{file_path:$f}} + (if $w == "" then {} else {cwd:$w} end)'
  fi
}
# the hook's exit code for one tool call, run from the scratch working directory
wpa_rc() { local input; input="$(wpa_in "$1" "$2")"; ( cd "$wpa_cwd" && printf '%s' "$input" | env "${WPA_ENV[@]}" "$WPA_HOOK" >/dev/null 2>&1; echo $? ); }
# the hook's stderr (the BLOCKED text), followed by a final `WPA_RC=<exit code>` line
wpa_err() { local input; input="$(wpa_in "$1" "$2")"; ( cd "$wpa_cwd" && printf '%s' "$input" | env "${WPA_ENV[@]}" "$WPA_HOOK" 2>&1 >/dev/null; echo "WPA_RC=$?" ); }
# a case = begin + checks + end_case; it counts once, a failure names the first six checks that are off
wpa_begin_bash_case() { _wpa_name="$1"; _wpa_bad=""; _wpa_nbad=0; }
wpa_x() {   # <tool> <want exit> <payload>
  local rc p="${3//$wpa_nl/ }"; rc="$(wpa_rc "$1" "$3")"
  if [[ "$rc" != "$2" ]]; then
    _wpa_nbad=$((_wpa_nbad + 1))
    [[ $_wpa_nbad -le 6 ]] && _wpa_bad="$_wpa_bad {$1 ${p:0:72}: want exit $2, got $rc}"
  fi
  return 0
}
wpa_chk() {   # <label> <want> <got>
  if [[ "$2" != "$3" ]]; then
    _wpa_nbad=$((_wpa_nbad + 1))
    [[ $_wpa_nbad -le 6 ]] && _wpa_bad="$_wpa_bad {$1: want '$2', got '$3'}"
  fi
  return 0
}
wpa_end_case() {
  if [[ $_wpa_nbad -eq 0 ]]; then pass=$((pass + 1))
  else fail=$((fail + 1)); failures+=("FAIL [$_wpa_name]: $_wpa_nbad check(s) off:$_wpa_bad"); fi
}
wpa_all_bash_case() { local name="$1" want="$2" c; shift 2; wpa_begin_bash_case "$name"; for c in "$@"; do wpa_x Bash "$want" "$c"; done; wpa_end_case; }   # <name> <want> <command>...
wpa_all_read_case() { local name="$1" want="$2" c; shift 2; wpa_begin_bash_case "$name"; for c in "$@"; do wpa_x Read "$want" "$c"; done; wpa_end_case; }   # <name> <want> <path>...
wpa_all_edit_case() { local name="$1" want="$2" c; shift 2; wpa_begin_bash_case "$name"; for c in "$@"; do wpa_x Edit "$want" "$c"; done; wpa_end_case; }   # <name> <want> <path>...
wpa_one_bash_case() { local name="$1"; wpa_begin_bash_case "$name"; wpa_x "$2" "$3" "$4"; wpa_end_case; }                                           # <name> <tool> <want> <payload>
# one-shot ACK for a Read/Edit block: SECRET_GUARD_ACK_PATH + a nonce (and, when <marker> is yes, the marker file the hook consumes)
wpa_ack_env() {   # <path> <marker: yes|no>  -> appends to WPA_ENV
  local nonce="wpa$(date +%s)$RANDOM"
  WPA_ENV=("${WPA_ENV[@]}" "SECRET_GUARD_ACK_PATH=$1" "SECRET_GUARD_ACK_NONCE=$nonce")
  [[ "$2" == yes ]] && : > "/tmp/secret-guard-ack-${USER:-anon}-${nonce}.nonce"
  wpa_ack_nonce="$nonce"
}
wpa_ack_cleanup() { rm -f "/tmp/secret-guard-ack-${USER:-anon}-${wpa_ack_nonce:-none}.nonce"; wpa_use_none; }
